ParlaMint-IT_2015-01-22-LEG17-Senato-sed-382.ana.u590	DE PETRIS (Misto-SEL). Madam President, the request made by Senator Fornaro is of absolute common sense, partly because we know perfectly well that both the text of Senator Finocchiaro's first amendment and the text presented in this House (I see now that there is another version, through a sub-amendment by Senator Marcucci) try to speak on the well-known issue of pinball. But we also know that they are simply attempts to limit their effect. Also in the current law there was a certain degree of unpredictability, but it was limited, because the number of circumscriptions was limited (there were 26 circumscriptions). Today we are in a very different situation, because we have one hundred multi-name colleges, within the Circumscription. And, therefore, the mechanism is very complicated and means that the forces that have an allocation of seats, even with these corrections - I understand - will not have any possibility of predicting where it can actually trigger the seat (I see that Senator Quagliariello, who knows the situation, makes a nod with his hand). This, of course, applies especially to smaller forces. All the more reason, therefore, precisely because we are faced with non-trivial technical data, which can have a distorting effect that we are trying to limit, we should have a simulation of what might happen, both with the text presented before and with the modified one. Senator Fornaro's request seems to me, therefore, of absolute common sense. I repeat that we are always within the scope of limitation, because this aspect was already present in the Porcellum, but the unpredictability was very limited. Taking - for example - my political strength, with the law in force only in two cases seats have taken place in places where it was absolutely not predictable could take place. In this text, however, the system is still highly distorting, because everything happens in one hundred colleges within the districts, so there is really a pinball risk, because they go up and then you don't know where they fall. We should try to understand exactly what the spill-over effect is in order to be able to think seriously and decide how to vote and what further changes to make.
ParlaMint-IT_2015-01-22-LEG17-Senato-sed-382.ana.u591	I'm asking to talk.
ParlaMint-IT_2015-01-22-LEG17-Senato-sed-382.ana.u592	He has the right, if the senators in front of her move (Some senators speak in the hemicycle covering the view of Senator Quagliariello, who changes the bench). Is she moving? But that doesn't seem right, you know? I call the Presidents Schifani and Azzolini back. (The senators leave the hemicycle.) Thank you. He asked to intervene Senator Quagliariello, who moved to the senator's benches for life. Mr Quegliariello (AP (NCD-UDC)). Madam President, I would like to say to my fellow Members, Mr De Petris and Mr Fornaro, that there is obviously no need to go into more detail, but I would also like to try to explain that the decision, more than technical, is political and probably more necessary to understand the mechanism than a simulation. Trying to simplify things as much as possible, the seats are of three types: those that are allocated by full quotient, those that are assigned for the higher remains and the so-called surplus seats. Senator De Petris has quite well understood that this problem is very wide, because colleges are one hundred and the barrier has fallen to 3%. In theory, the small forces could find themselves in almost all surplus seats. We are not talking about large numbers, but very limited numbers that, for small forces, can be almost total. The surplus seats are - if you pass me the image - those pieces that you put to complete the puzzle when it is already complete. It is those seats that the larger forces surrender to the smaller forces. The larger forces obviously surrender those seats in the colleges where they have the smaller remains. What's going on? If we want to enforce the territorial criterion, and therefore fully respect it, the smaller forces come to find those seats in the colleges in which the larger forces lose them. And, therefore, those colleges may also be assigned their candidates who have taken less votes. For the smaller forces, therefore, this law becomes a real roulette, where surplus seats can be assigned where it happens, without any justice of numbers. Of course, a law like this cannot be done, especially when we have, theoretically, lowered the threshold for access to Parliament to 3%. The amendment we are debating, then, provides that the largest forces will lose - as it should be - the seats where they have the smallest remains, but the forces that acquire those seats will not take them in the same colleges, but where they have the highest quotients. What can happen is that the territorial framework is not fully respected and that in some colleges there is an additional seat and in others one less seat. But we are always talking about no more than one seat less or more. In this way, however, predictability is respected and it does not happen that in a small force, which has 3, 4 or 5 percent, maybe someone who has a percentage that can also be half or a third of another partymate of his who is present in the same constituency. Therefore, the choice is of a political nature: we assert either the list of territory or the ranking of party, and this always and only for the surplus seats. The amendments that have been tabled propose that the party ranking be applied, so that this law does not become a roulette for anyone.
ParlaMint-IT_2015-01-22-LEG17-Senato-sed-382.ana.u593	[[Applause from the FI-PdL Group XVII and the Senators Marcucci and Collina.]]
ParlaMint-IT_2015-01-22-LEG17-Senato-sed-382.ana.u594	I'm asking to talk.
ParlaMint-IT_2015-01-22-LEG17-Senato-sed-382.ana.u595	You have the right to do so.
ParlaMint-IT_2015-01-22-LEG17-Senato-sed-382.ana.u596	CRIMI (M5S). Madam President, we are here to explain the amendments, I understand. PRESIDENT. - The debate is This is Amendment 1,7000/1 (text 2)/100. CRIMI (M5S). Before we proceed to the explanation of vote on that amendment, I would like to explain the three sub-amendments I have tabled. I think it's planned, so I'm not asking for anything transcendental. PRESIDENT. - The debate is closed. You're welcome to introduce them. CRIMI (M5S). In sub-amendment 1,7000/1 (text 2)/101 we entrusted the National Central Office with the identification of criteria and methods, simply including the forecast that this Office will intervene in the distribution of seats. In sub-amendment 1,7000/1 (text 2)/102 we have foreseen a situation that can be provocative: the National Central Office proposes to the constitutionally competent bodies that a new election be made. We have tabled this provocative amendment because this electoral law, as designed, creates a multiplier effect of that flipper effect. The flipper effect, of which many have spoken so far, leads to a distortion of the will of the voters and of the territorial connection and knowledge of the candidates. In this law, the flipper effect is multiplied by the presence of plurinomial colleges within the districts: this is the key element that needs to be healed. The only way to minimise this problem is to divide the allocation of seats into one hundred multi-name colleges - I am giving a suggestion to the rapporteurs of this law - not divided into districts. Why did we have to come up with these container mechanisms and under containers? Why have large circuits within which there will be four, five or six colleges, depending on the size of the circuits, in which this assignment roulette will proceed? Therefore, it already has the first effect in the allocation to the districts; then within the districts additional effects, having lower numbers (because there you start not from the total of the Members, but from the number assigned to that district), even more distorting. That is why it is wrong to set up this law which provides for cascade mechanisms one after the other. Finally, I would like to outline an amendment which seeks to restore the voter's will. This is an amendment whereby, for the purposes of the operations referred to in the previous period, account is taken of the number of preferences obtained by candidates on each deficit list: that is to say, the deficit list on the list within which the non-elected candidate has the highest number of preferences. This means that, at least in a residual way, in extreme cases, the will of the elector to identify the candidate to be brought to Parliament is respected, a will that has been trampled on until now. [[Senator Zanda points out that he wants to intervene.]]

ParlaMint-IT_2015-01-22-LEG17-Senato-sed-382.ana.u625	Mr Paglini (M5S). Madam President, do you know what a bathroom is? So began yesterday Daniela Rombi, president of the association of relatives of the 32 victims of the massacre of Viareggio in 2009, speaking for the first time at the trial of Lucca from the witness stand, in front of the platoon of the defence of the 42 accused. I remember that the most famous of the defendants is Mauro Moretti, former CEO of the State Railways, wanted by Renzi (after the massacre) as chief of Finmeccanica. Moretti is knowledge of the former Florentine mayor in past times, as he had begun to weave relationships with him for the issue of high speed in Florence and for the management of the case company transports ATAF. Daniela Rombi in the massacre lost Emanuela, 21 years old, died with 98 percent of the body burned, and yesterday in the trial, addressed to the judge, began saying: Do you know what a bathroom is? A bathroom is this: they put you in a bathtub with liquid on purpose and rub you to take away the infected tissue, which is now dead. They clean up well and sanitize the whole body. But it's a very intrusive action, because patients lose a lot of blood, a lot of fluids, so they also have to be comfortable to face a "bath" because then they fall with the temperature at 34 degrees. It helps but it devastates a lot, the physicist suffers a lot. Emanuela had her hair cut to zero. I made her a crochet hat, because I couldn't see it like that. They only put it on him when she was dressed in the coffin. Mrs. Rombi yesterday told those forty-two days, the four operations and the two "baths" suffered by her child. Emanuela is one of the many people killed in that massacre and for each of them there is a story of infinite pain. During the trial, after the oath, Daniela Rombi requested thirty seconds of silence in honor of the victims, but it was not possible, it was not granted. Madam President, I would like to inform you that my 5 Star Movement Group and I will use thirty-two seconds of my time to gather here in silence, thirty-two seconds of respect, thirty-two seconds of our lives, to honor the 32 victims awaiting justice. I will finish my speech then, at the end of silence, reminding us that the life of a person is worth much more than a second! [[The Assembly stands and observes a pause for recollection.]]
ParlaMint-IT_2015-01-22-LEG17-Senato-sed-382.ana.u626	You're right. I thank you and the House.

ParlaMint-IT_2015-01-22-LEG17-Senato-sed-382.ana.u630	LUMIA (PD). Madam President, I would ask you, please, to urge Mr Guidi on what is happening to the AnsaldoBreda Group, Finmeccanica. Today, after several days, the workers of the Carini plant of this group have come back to protest. We know, Madam President, that a debate is taking place to sell the AnsaldoBreda group complex with all the factories. From this point of view it would also be important to debate in the Committees of Merit and I think also in the Chamber, because another important piece of industrial production of our country risks to go away, not because there are no orders, not because there is no market, but because this group has not been able to organize itself in a managerial and qualified way in order to be able to respond also on the level of accounts to the slices of production that it has been able to conquer in Italy, in Europe and in the world. I would particularly like to draw your attention, Madam President, to an issue concerning Carini. There production has always been serious and qualified. What's going on? This year 35 employees were seconded to Reggio Calabria and now, without notice, 15 other workers have been transferred to the site of Naples. In short, 50 travelers who were assigned to two other establishments and who chose not to follow this imposition that was given to them. Do you know why, Madam President? Because the risk is that, in the meantime that the new buyer is located (you are talking to the Chinese giant Insignia and the Japanese giant Hitachi, as the press reports), you want to close the Breda-Ansaldo plant in Carini in Palermo. That would be a joke. That is why, Madam President, I would ask you to speak to Mr Guidi and ask him to come here. You must avoid this mockery, keep together all four plants (Pistoia, Naples, Reggio Calabria and Carini) and make sure that overall this group evaluates the possibility of relocating these plants together and not excluding just the Sicilian one of Carini. PRESIDENT. - The debate is closed. All right, we'll push.

ParlaMint-IT_2015-01-22-LEG17-Senato-sed-382.ana.u633	CANDIANI (LN-Aut). Madam President, my speech is intended to bring again to the attention of Mr Guidi, through his person, the story that the Gallazzi society of Tradate is experiencing. As I mentioned at yesterday's sitting, the company is at the end of a controlled administration process, with a paradoxical situation that becomes worse day by day. In the specific case, the company has 2,500,000 euros in cash and must pay 600,000 to its suppliers; but the two commissioners that the Ministry has appointed on 24 December have not yet set foot in the company and have not yet given the company the opportunity to pay its suppliers; with an objective result: the company is losing market shares - we remember that it is a company that has 40% of the market shares only in the field of adhesive tapes - because the suppliers do not give materials and customers no longer trust. It is a situation that must be unlocked; there can be no solution in wasting time. In addition, Madam President, there is a further aggravating factor. The Extraordinary Commissioner assigned the company to an American company, following a regular tender. Pre-assigned by the notary, but the two commissioners appointed by the Ministry, with a stroke of lightning on December 24, are calling everything into question, even wanting to re-launch the tender. The question that comes to us is: what interest is being pursued? The interest of the workers of the enterprise or instead that of who wants to make the enterprise fail, in order to let the competitors eat it? The serious doubt is that bureaucracy and the inability to make decisions are causing irreparable damage. We call for this to be brought to the attention of Mr Guidi in a serious way. PRESIDENT. - The debate is closed. I'll take care of it.

ParlaMint-IT_2015-01-22-LEG17-Senato-sed-382.ana.u636	Barani (GAL (GS, LA-nS, MPA, NPSI, PPI)). Madam President, I am speaking because yesterday, at the end of the House's work, Mr Puglia reported - and of course I was very alarmed - that the last of the last (disabled people) had been abandoned in Campania. Of course I wanted to inform myself and I want it to remain in the record that what my colleague Mr Puglia said yesterday does not respond to truth, because the Campania Region does not provide for any cuts to places for disabled people, but simply the conversion of rehabilitation places to assisted health residences. As national legislation establishes, no increase of places for rehabilitation was allowed and therefore, in order to support the families, the Region and Governor Caldoro have planned the conversion, just to leave no family in difficulty. The agreement with the ASL and the structures has already been successfully concluded in the last hours for the Provinces of Avellino and Benevento, and between today and tomorrow the agreements will also be concluded with all the other Provinces. The conversion programme, however, also provides for an accompanying phase until the conversions or activations are completed. Against the closing of 1,530 semi-residential places for rehabilitation for mentally disabled people, 3,170 beds will be activated in health care residences (RSA) and day centres for disabled persons not self-sufficient, of which 1,902 places of residential type and 1,268 semi-residential. The offer of places to be activated is therefore much greater than that of rehabilitation subject to conversion, thus greatly widening the number of disabled enlistable and, ultimately, with a better satisfaction of the levels of assistance as well as greater appropriateness. The maneuver is not dictated by demands of economic order, but it fits in a broader strategy of rebalancing of the offer that the Region would have had to pursue also independently from the plan of return. Yesterday morning the city council member for social policies Bianca D'Angelo and the general director of health Mario Vasco went to the square to dialogue with the associations present. The Region is ready at any time for dialogue with the citizens and will always be at their side against the cuts provided by the Government. However, the Agency will not leave anyone at home, despite the severe reduction of funds that penalizes and will penalise even more assistance in the future.

Madam President, I came at the last moment to speak and not to go against Senator Stefano - and I call him by name - Esposito, but because citizens must understand and see behind the news. Senator Stefano Esposito, who presented a bill against the nominations (while today we have shown that through his amendment we will find ourselves a Parliament of nominations), sent us the following message at the time: I send the text to all the senators, especially those who support the bill to eliminate the nominations, especially those of the 5 Star Movement. We want to understand from them whether they really want to move from words to deeds. They have been elected in the wake of indignation against the political class and must now give a response to the citizens. Well, now Esposito gives you an answer to the citizens, you write one thing and then you do another. But that is not the reason for my speech. I took the trouble to see the Democratic Party's charter of intent, with which the PD presented itself to the citizens and I realized that Senator Stefano Esposito's behavior is the behaviour of the Democratic Party. In fact, under the heading "Democracy" of this Charter you write: We must defeat the ideology of the end of the politics and prodigious virtues of a man only in command. You did this: you put one man in command! In another important step it is proposed to eradicate the idea that the response of unemployment can be in the devaluation and devalued work: you have approved the jobs act, that is the exact opposite! In point 5 of the same Charter, the following is inserted under the heading "Equality": In the next legislature, the issue will have to be addressed from the point of view of the dignity and rights of all and no longer of the powerful in the search for impunity.Well, fiscal delegation seems to me to go completely to the other side. Under the heading "Common good" you should remember that: "The 2011 referendums affirmed the principle of water as a non-privatizable good": water must not be public for you. Finally, under the heading "Responsibilities" of this document you affirm that those who will have the burden and the honor to lead the majority will have the responsibility of a slim government composition and taken away from the logic of partition: exactly what you are doing with the delinquent Silvio Berlusconi!
